Compiled by PARAS Sdn Bhd from its Planting Advisory (PA) and Agronomy reports. Figures as reported.

HOW TO USE THIS FILE (instructions for the AI assistant)
- Answer questions about this one estate using ONLY the report extracts below.
- Lead with the direct answer, in short numbered points.
- After each figure, cite the report in brackets, e.g. (PA 2/2026) or (Agronomy 1/2026).
- Always state the period a yield figure covers (e.g. Jan–Jun 2026).
- Fertiliser doses in grams per palm. Money in RM.
- If the extracts do not contain the answer, say so plainly. Do not guess.
- Where two reports disagree, give both figures.

REPORT EXTRACTS
TENGKARASAN ESTATE, Meridian Plantations Sdn Bhd (United Malacca Berhad group, UMB). Fact pack drawn from PA Report 2/2026 (Planting Advisor Mr. Boey Chee Weng; visit 15-16 October 2026; review period May to September 2026, the first 5 months of financial year FY2026/27, which runs May 2026 to April 2027) and Agronomy Report 2-2026 (Mr. Alison Janari, Junior Agronomist, and Mr. Chong Kuan Ming, Senior Agronomist; visit 30 June 2026; manuring programme 2026/2027). Senior Manager Mr. Phor Kok Beng. Manager Mr. Lawrence Saidin (since 15.03.2021). Previous PA 1/2026 (visit 29-30 April 2026, May 2025-March 2026, 11 months): 47,002.46 t FFB, 22.14 t/ha vs 19.70 budget, cost RM331/t vs RM384 budget.

AREA (PA 2/2026 section 2, as at September 2026): mature 2,229.74 ha (88.50%), immature 70.07 ha (2.78%), total oil palm 2,299.81 ha; non-cultivated 219.64 ha (all roads 124.54, football field 0.65, building sites and roads 10.03, water catchment/ponds 2.15, streams/drains 8.24, others 11.94, unplantable 62.09); total per title 2,519.45 ha. Mature up from 2,122.65 ha and immature down from 129.11 ha (PA 2.1-2.2); no replanting schedule for 2026. This now matches Agro 2-2026 Table 1 (June 2026). Appendix 1 (Sep 2026) lists 289,921 palms, 126 SPH; sub-blocks by planting: 1999A 233.72 ha (136 SPH), 1999C 89.73 (120), 2000A 134.67 (132), 2002B 89.63 (142), 2002A 476.77 (A23-A24 54.80 ha 123 SPH; A25-A35 421.97 ha 130), 2003A 108.59 (139), 2003B 126.46 (132), 2003C 55.79 (131), 2003D 63.79 (146), 2003E 179.94 (140), 2003F 105.88 (83), 2003G 32.80 (122), 2003H 83.88 (98), 2005A 31.26 (145), 2006A 58.44 (151), 2006C 9.03 (146), 2006D 25.47 (142), 2007A 32.80 (141), 2008A 56.82 (114), 2013 C66/C77 76.14 (130), 2014 D71/D72 77.19 (135), 2015 B67/B68/B70/B78 plus B82 (listed 2024, peat) 80.94 (137); immature 2024 B85 20.23 ha, B88 34.69 ha, B89 15.15 ha. Planting material IOI. Blocks 73, 74, 75, 76 transferred to PNE.
Agro 2-2026: block 23A-82 (20.95 ha) declared mature for FY26/27; immature P24A-85 20.23 ha, P24A-88 34.69 ha, P26A-89 15.15 ha at land preparation (June 2026). Reclaimed supply areas (blocks 63, 45, 46) merged into main sub-blocks; Agro wants them kept separate. Palm census (Agro Table 3): 302,139 palms (Dec 2025, 135 SPH) to 303,933 (Jun 2026, 132 SPH); 03B 159 to 128 SPH (vacant points suspected Ganoderma), 03A 155 to 137, 05A 164 to 145. Replanting: 99A01-99A04 (118.99 ha) FY29/30; 99A05-99A80 (107.2 ha) FY30/31; 99C (89.73 ha) deferred to FY29/30.

LABOUR (PA 2/2026 section 3, September 2026): 194 workers vs 216 required. Harvester/carriers 119 vs 123 (1 per 18 ha needed; 1:19 ha actual; short 4, adequate if mechanised in-field collection works); 10 workers to be interviewed for harvesting and spraying in October. Manuring 17 vs 18 (adequate per text), spraying 15 vs 20 (short 5), mandores 3 + 1, general workers 15 vs 27 (functions must be clear to avoid high overhead), tractor drivers 5. Worker to planted area 1:12 ha, satisfactory. Staff adequate: Senior Assistant En. Samsuddin resigned August; Mr. Veron transferred from Paitan Estate in August; Field Conductor Mr. John Francis resigned October. CEO view: labour sufficient if placed by skill and trained; PA: still short of harvesters and sprayers.

GENERAL CHARGES (PA section 5, May-Sep 2026, FY2026/27): grand total RM2,390,948 vs budget to date RM2,511,055 (RM1,040/ha vs RM1,114/ha), underspent 5%, including depreciation RM1,108,940 (budget RM1,129,840). Staff salary and related RM372,706 (budget 393,490). Labour overhead RM375,200 (budget 510,796): paid leave 151,479, welfare 63,292, upkeep lines/electricity/water 54,280 vs 3,540 (repairs to labour quarters, under-budgeted), recruitment 48,407 (budget 191,395), EPF 24,120, SOCSO/EIS 13,278, OSHA/DOSH 13,400. Office and related RM493,967 (budget 440,224): water/electricity 336,970 vs 256,140 (+32%; genset rental and overhaul of 3 gensets, unbudgeted), transport and travelling 47,371 (+51%), general office 32,338, security 23,144 (+8%, charges from Paitan Estate), insurance 22,383. Premises upkeep RM40,135 (other building maintenance +29%, unbudgeted store extension).

UPKEEP AND CULTIVATION (PA section 6, to September 2026, mature 2,230 ha, FFB 17,456 t vs budget 21,895 t): total RM3,405,805 vs RM3,178,105 (RM1,527/ha vs 1,465; RM195/t vs 145), overspent 7%. Lines (RM, RM/ha, RM/t actual vs budget): manuring material 1,608,866 (722/ha; 92 vs 57/t); roads and bridges 732,247 (328/ha; 42 vs 25/t); selective spraying and lallang 258,538 (116 vs 70/ha, +70%); circle 169,265 (76 vs 38/ha, +106%); composting contract 155,245 (-41%); manuring labour 116,376 (+48%); pruning 96,577 (-28%); pests and diseases 62,891 (+55%); composting material 62,449 (-84%); soil/water conservation 54,572 (-59%); manuring transport 28,577; supervision 20,767; drains 8,690 (-74%).
Circle spraying (PA 6.2): 1,605 ha (0.7 rounds), 58% of 2,780 ha (1.2 rounds) budgeted to September; some blocks had clean circles so spraying was deferred; circles generally clean. RB16 pumps, high volume, 4.3 ha/man-day; piece rate RM16/ha, earnings RM68.80/day vs daily wage RM65.38. Glyphosate 1.26 l/ha (8.4 pumps/man-day as printed); pumps per man-day not verifiable: time and motion study advised. PA advises CDA sprayers; budget to come in the next budgeting exercise. By field (3 rounds a year, % of the year): 1999A 29-33%, 1999C 63-67%, 2002A blocks 32-34 63-67%, 2003E 32-66%, 2003D 32-33%; none yet in 2003C, 2003F, 2003H, 2014D, 2006D, 2008A, C77, A23-A28 and parts of 2003A, 2003B, 2003G, 2002B and 2015B.
Selective spraying (PA 6.3): 2,108.23 ha (1 round), 95% of 2,217.78 ha (1 round) budgeted to September; inter rows satisfactory with very little Asystasia, small jungle woodies and VOPs; sprayed VOPs satisfactory. Knapsack power sprayers 3.3 ha/man-day; RM20/ha, earnings RM66/day. Garlon 1.32 l/ha, occasionally Ally 0.11 kg/ha, equivalent to 8.8 pumps/ha (high). Mist blowers could give 5-6 ha/man-day. Abandoned area at P02/B2 needs rehabilitation.
Manuring (PA 6.4, Appendix 2): 3,796.22 of 4,790.79 ha budgeted (79%) to September, delayed by late delivery of RP and NK2. Broadcasting 25 bags/man-day, RM17/ha, RM85/day. Sub-surface application at P06D/61 (25.47 ha): 1.6 ha and 8 bags per man-day (low), RM17/ha, earnings RM27.20/day; reassess the method (CEO: consider semi-mechanised). EFB on 123 of 548 ha budgeted (insufficient EFB in the low cropping season) at 40 t/ha; contract trucks 120 t/day; excavator levelling 96 t/day at RM5/t; mill-to-field transport RM5.65/t (0.49 km) to RM15.50/t (15-19.99 km). PA Appendix 2 programme (g/palm): 1999A NK2 2,500 Jun, Oct, Feb (applied Aug 2026); 2000A NK2 2,500, 2,500, 2,000; 1999C none ('Due to Replanting fy27/28'); 2002A, 2002B, 2003A, 2003B, 2003C, 2003G NK1 2,000 May and Aug, 1,700 Dec and Mar; 2003D, 2003E, 2003F, 2003H NK1 2,500 May and Aug, 1,700 Dec and Mar; 2005A, 2006A, 2006C Mix A 2,500 Jun, NK1 2,500 Sep, Mix A 2,000 Jan, NK1 2,000 Apr; 2007A, 2008A Mix A 2,500 Jun, BRP 750 Jul, Mix C 2,000 Sep, Mix B 2,000 Jan, Mix C 2,000 Apr; 2013C, 2014D, 2015B Mix B 2,500 May, BRP 750 Jul, Mix B 2,200 Aug, Nov, Mar. First rounds applied Jul-Aug 2026; second rounds Sep-Oct 2026 in most NK1 fields, none printed for 2003B, 20, 37, 54; Mix C (Sep) and Mix B (Aug) rounds not printed as applied.
Pests and diseases (PA 6.5): one round of rat baiting at all blocks, 2,084.35 of 2,208.79 ha programmed (May-Sep 2026); no follow-up census; census every 3 months, bait above 10% damage; very little damage seen. Ganoderma census in progress: P13C/77 10%, P14D/72 6%, P14D/71 7.4%; infected palms seen in P03/56.
Pruning (PA 6.6): 2,331 of 8,835 ha budgeted progressively pruned and paid (table: 2,356.4 of 8,835.16 ha, 26.67%); by field 2003E 45.7%, 2003A 44.2%, 2003H 43.5%, 2014D 41.6%, 1999A 26.4%, 2008A 5.4%, 2006D 0%. Appearance satisfactory. Rates per palm per year: RM1.20 (1999-2003), RM1.10 (2005-2007), RM0.80 (2013), RM0.70 (2014-2015).
Roads, drains, other (PA 6.7-6.10): 1,769 chains graded vs 1,566 budgeted, RM29/chain (budget RM29, incl. compaction); crusher run on 520 of 1,031 chains, RM553 vs RM623/chain; 4,161 MT crusher run at RM60/MT; roads very satisfactory. Desilting 8x6x4 13 of 436 chains, 6x4x3 11 of 1,013, 4x3x2 31 of 403 (RM56, 44, 28/chain as budgeted); desilting satisfactory. One old water gate to be replaced this financial year. 291 palms supplied to rehabilitated areas (A63, C66), in progress.

HARVESTING (PA section 7, to September 2026): RM1,382,342.60 vs budget to date RM1,763,224.02 = RM79.19/t vs 80.53 (supervision 4.16 vs 4.73; harvesting labour 58.57 vs 66.21; internal transport 15.46 vs 8.72; tools 1.00 vs 0.87); annual budget printed RM2,576,529 (RM48.36/t). 11.3 rounds vs 15 budgeted (below requirement); interval averaged 14 days; outturn 59%, because some harvesters work without attending muster so attendance is not recorded (to resolve with the Senior Manager). 16 crawlers (3 cutters + 1 collector each) plus 5 EV crawlers; wheelbarrows elsewhere. Platform collection by one contractor and estate tractors; external transport by bins outsourced, but FFB double-handled at stumping points; scissor-lift trailers bought, so double handling should stop; try Super Bull tyred tractors. Harvest rates (RM/t, t/man-day, earnings/day, basic RM65.38): 1999 55, 2.26, 124.30; 2000 54, 1.50, 81.00; 2002 53, 1.94, 102.82; 2003 52, 2.20, 114.40; 2005 50, 2.31, 115.50; 2006 49, 2.15, 105.35; 2007 47, 2.45, 115.15; 2008 46, 1.76, 80.96; 2013 43, 2.57, 110.51; 2014-2015 42, 1.92, 80.64. Grabbers 40-65 t/day at RM13.40/t; manual loading 30 t/day at RM5.50/t. CEO: aim for 3.0 t/man-day.

CROP (PA section 8, May-Sep 2026, FY2026/27): FFB 17,456.09 t vs budget to date 21,895.00 t (annual budget 53,279.00 t) on 2,229.74 ha; same period last year (May-Sep 2025) 22,413.49 t on 2,122.7 ha. Yield 7.83 t/ha vs 10.03 budget (22% below) and 10.56 last year (26% below); annual budget 24.41 t/ha. 'Annual 12 months budget' last year 45,874.00 t (21.58 t/ha) is the FY2025/26 budget, not actual. CPO 3,376.47 t, OER 19.34% vs 19.06% budget (last year 18.52%); kernel 867.45 t, KER 4.97%; CPO yield 1.51 t/ha vs 1.91. Shortfall from a low cropping season in the first half of the FY. Bunch weight Sep 2026 vs Sep 2025 (kg): 1999 19.8 vs 18.0; 2000 18.2 vs 16.4; 2002 18.3 vs 16.2; 2003 18.9 vs 15.4; 2005 17.2 vs 13.5; 2006 17.4 vs 15.0; 2007 19.7 vs 15.6; 2008 15.9 vs 13.6; 2013 14.6 vs 12.6; 2014 12.4 vs 11.1; 2015 13.9 vs 11.9; PA thinks the 2025 bunch counts were inaccurate.

PRODUCTION COST (PA section 9, May-Sep 2026, RM/t FFB actual vs budget to date): general charges 71 vs 61; upkeep and cultivation 195 vs 145; harvesting and collection 91 vs 90; total ex-estate 357 vs 296. Per tonne CPO 367 vs 317, 1,009 vs 762, 471 vs 474, total 1,847 vs 1,553. High because crop fell short of budget. Note: section 7 gives harvesting RM79.19/t, not 91; general charges 71 matches cash charges (excluding depreciation) allocated to mature area.
VEHICLES (PA section 10): many tractors had high repair costs (Landini RM35.53/hr vs 17.62; rented tractor RM50.28 vs 26.48; grabbers RM20-37/hr vs 12-13); big gensets RM290,390 (RM52.55/hr vs 35.09). 2 electric 3-wheelers went to Marmahat Estate and 3 electric crawlers to Peninsula. CAPEX RM353,445 vs RM1,378,846 (underspent): 3 scissor-lift trailers RM63,600, 2 tractors RM260,000, water tank stands, genset 10KVA RM9,500; crusher run RM652,029, bund block 38/39 (1,128 m) RM156,792 and tidal gates RM30,025 in progress.
IMMATURE (PA section 12): PR24/88/85/86/84/68/70/45/46 (54.84 ha): no circle spraying though 329 ha budgeted (circles clean); selective 82.36 ha (1.5 rounds), 34% of 239.59 ha (4 rounds); manuring done to September; good fruit sets; no pests; 73 palms supplied. PR26/89B ex-Bakau (15 ha): no spraying budget, manuring not due, appearance satisfactory, 10 palms supplied. PR25/69 (15 ha) needs little supply.
CEO COMMENTS ON PA 1/2026 and PA 2/2026 responses: circle spraying to use CDA; selective by mist blower every 4 months; sub-surface manuring to be re-evaluated; rat census after baiting, Ganoderma records and deboling (census continuing, rat damage not significant); progressive pruning consistent; road grading prioritised (roads very satisfactory); desilting satisfactory; water gate replacement; supplying in rehab areas; productivity 3.0 t/man-day, stop double handling; extend contracts to manuring and weeding; tractor running costs high; main issue: low sprayer productivity.
RAINFALL (PA Appendix 3, calendar): 2025 4,126 mm/204 days; Jan-Sep 2026 2,459 mm/107 days (Jan 325, Feb 743, Mar 330, Apr 119, May 133, Jun 284, Jul 204, Aug 65, Sep 256 mm).

AGRONOMY 2-2026 MANURING: FY25/26 programme fully completed only in June 2026; NK1 and Mix A rounds due May 2025 applied Jul-Aug 2025 (2-3 months late), Mix C due Aug 2025 applied Dec 2025 (4 months). FY26/27 at July 2026: 508.68 of 773.88 mt due applied (66%); NK1 404.29 mt 100%, Mix B 69%, NK2 33%, Mix A, RP and Auruma not started; immature CPD 63%. Application quality acceptable. Carbon fertiliser trial in P15-78 from 4 April 2026.
Agro FY26/27 programme (Appendix 1, grams per palm): 99A and 00A NK2 2,200 x3 (Jun, Oct, Feb); 99C NK2 2,200 x2 (Nov, Mar); 02A, 02B, 03A-03H NK1 2,000 x3 (May, Aug, Dec) plus NK1 1,500 (Mar; G42 1,200); 05A, 06A, 06C, 07A, 08A Mix A 2,000 (Jun), NK1 2,000 (Sep, Jan), Mix A 2,000 (Apr); 06D Mix A 8,000 sub-soil (Jun); 13C, 14D, 15B Mix B 2,200 (May, Aug, Nov, Mar) plus RP 1,500 (Jun); immature 23A, 85, 88 CPD 1,200 x3, 1,500 (Feb), RP 2,000 (Apr). FY27/28 programme formulated (Appendix 2). Mix A 45% AS/40% MOP/15% kieserite; Mix B 1% borate; NK1 50% AS/50% MOP; NK2 60% SOA/40% MOP.
EFB (Agro section 4, FY25/26): programme 23,994.61 mt; received 24,389.82 mt; applied 21,890.95 mt (stated 89%); rates up to 98 mt/ha vs 40 mt/ha standard in some blocks while others got none. Avoid thick EFB in young mature (Rhinoceros beetle).
AGRONOMIC OBSERVATIONS (Agro section 5): harvesting intervals over 15 days in May-Jul 2026 in 02A, 03A, 03D, 03E, 03F, 03H, 05A, 06A, 06C, 07A, 08A, 13C, 14D, 15B (03D 25 days, 06A 14-28). Platforms 97-100% ripe (blocks 80, 1); oxidised loose fruit in block 66. Leaf analysis (Table 10): N 2.61-2.86%; P 0.160-0.179%; K 0.97-1.16% marginal in several blocks, prioritise K; Mg and B adequate; Ca low, check soil Ca and acidity. Block 78 (peat) poor: keep water table 50-75 cm. Blocks 56, 57, 66 (and 63) recovering from waterlogging. About 15 ha of block 38 under tidal-water rehabilitation. Pruning FY25/26 71%; FY26/27 69% at July 2026. Weed control FY25/26 circle 70%, selective 115%, VOPs 93%; FY26/27 at July 2026 circle 42%, inter-row 76%; woody weeds and Clidemia hirta; herbicide choice inconsistent. Blanket rat baiting May 2026, no census since. Ganoderma a concern in block 77; 2019 census: 02A 717 confirmed, 00A 295, 99C 245, 06A 161, 03B 114; no deboling or mounding yet. Young mature P23A: good growth, Clidemia/Asystasia/Melastoma, thick EFB, Turnera for IPM. Immature P24A/P26A: weeding delayed, past RB damage recovering.

YIELD (Agro section 8): FY2025/26 (May 2025-Apr 2026, Table 17) 24.38 t/ha vs 18.81 in FY2024/25 (+30%); best 03D 27.95, 99A 27.66, 03A 27.61; lowest 03G 17.95, 06D 18.96, 07A 19.29. FY2026/27 Period 2 (May-Jun 2026, Table 18; dashboard block yields): 3.22 t/ha vs 4.11 (-22%); 05A -64% (2.07), 14D -62% (1.85), 03A -49%, 03H -39%, 13C -38%; up: 06C 5.05 (+14%), 03B, 03G, 03C, 07A. No full-year FFB tonnage printed in either report.

RAINFALL (Agro Table 19, FY May-Apr): FY17/18 3,988 mm/173 days; FY18/19 2,142/117; FY19/20 2,398/166; FY20/21 3,829/205; FY21/22 3,775/205; FY22/23 3,643/179; FY23/24 2,822/145; FY24/25 3,961/203; FY25/26 3,454/175; FY26/27 May-Jul 595 mm/37 days.

PARAS INTERNAL NOTES: PA section 9 harvesting RM91/t does not match section 7 (RM79.19/t); PA Appendix 2 fertiliser programme differs from the Agro FY26/27 recommendation (doses and products); PA Appendix 1 shows very low stands in F55, F56, H54 (74-87 SPH) not seen in Appendix 2 or Agro Table 3.

TOP 5 YIELD-LIMITING FACTORS (ranked by likely cost in lost crop)
1. Slow harvesting rounds, low outturn: 11.3 rounds vs 15 budgeted and outturn 59% to Sep 2026 (PA); intervals over 15 days in 14 fields in May–Jul 2026, e.g. 03D 25 days (Agro). (PA 2/2026 s.7 · Agro 2/2026)
2. Manuring late and incomplete: FY2026/27 fertiliser on 3,796.22 of 4,790.79 ha (79%) to Sep 2026, RP and NK2 delivered late; EFB on 123 of 548 ha. FY25/26 rounds were 2–4 months late (Agro). (PA 2/2026 s.6.4 · Agro 2/2026)
3. Ganoderma and falling palm stand: Census so far: C77 10%, D72 6%, D71 7.4% (PA, Oct 2026). 03B fell from 159 to 128 SPH, vacant points suspected Ganoderma; no deboling or mounding yet (Agro, Jun 2026). (PA 2/2026 s.6.5 · Agro 2/2026)
4. Waterlogging, peat and tidal areas: Blocks 56, 57, 66 still recovering from waterlogging; block 78 on peat performing poorly; about 15 ha of block 38 under tidal-water rehabilitation (Agro, Jun 2026). (Agro 2/2026)
5. Marginal leaf potassium, low calcium: Leaf K 0.97–1.16%, marginal in several blocks, so K fertiliser is the priority; Ca low across blocks (Agro 2-2026 Table 10, Jun 2026 visit). (Agro 2/2026)

TOP 5 PA ISSUES (main matters raised in the latest PA report)
1. Crop well below budget: 17,456.09 t vs 21,895 t budget, May–Sep 2026; 7.83 t/ha vs 10.03 budget and 10.56 in May–Sep 2025, blamed on the low-crop first half. (PA 2/2026 s.8)
2. Cost per tonne over budget: RM357/t FFB vs RM296 budget, May–Sep 2026, because crop fell short; upkeep RM195 vs 145/t, overspent 7% (manuring material RM92 vs 57/t). (PA 2/2026 s.6, 9)
3. Low sprayer productivity: Circle 4.3 and selective 3.3 ha/man-day to Sep 2026 with 15 sprayers vs 20; Garlon 1.32 l/ha (8.8 pumps/ha) is high. PA: use CDA and mist blowers. (PA 2/2026 s.3, 6.2, 6.3, 13)
4. Harvesting rounds and attendance: 11.3 rounds vs 15 budgeted, 14-day interval and 59% outturn to Sep 2026, as harvesters work without muster; 119 harvesters vs 123. (PA 2/2026 s.3, 7)
5. Manuring and EFB behind: 3,796.22 of 4,790.79 ha (79%) fertilised to Sep 2026, RP and NK2 late; EFB on 123 of 548 ha; sub-surface at P06D/61 only 8 bags/man-day. (PA 2/2026 s.6.4)
